It never runs the pipeline itself. ([tatolab/streamlib](https://github.com/tatolab/streamlib/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-verifier.md) · ★6)
- **evidence-verifier**: Validate findings from Phase 2 — evidence grounding, cross-validation, confidence filtering ([Laiff/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/Laiff/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-review/agents/evidence-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **evidence-verifier**: Use when a single claim is about to become load-bearing — a benchmark number in an ADR, a regulatory obligation, a compatibility guarantee, a vendor SLA, a "framework X supports Y" assertion, or any statistic quoted into a decision. Attempts to refute the claim rather than confirm it, chases every citation to its origin, and returns refuted whenever the claim cannot be established. Do not use to review whole documents, audit code, or check style; it verifies one claim at a time. ([fedcal/foundry](https://github.com/fedcal/foundry/blob/HEAD/plugins/foundry-research/agents/evidence-verifier.md))
- **evidence-verifier**: Use when a sentence on a shipped page asserts what a paper found, when adding or editing a row in evidence_annotations.json, when validate_evidence_annotations.py fails C1–C5, or when bin/sweep_unlicensed_claims.py flags a line. Reads the paper's RESULTS via PubMed and stores a verbatim sourceSpan; when the span does not license the claim, reports the exact rewrite instead of trimming the span. Writes only evidence_annotations.json. ([jmoss333/psychiatry-clerkship](https://github.com/jmoss333/psychiatry-clerkship/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-verifier.md))
- **evidence-verifier**: Verifies or rejects candidate findings against the diff and repository context. Use after diff-analyst in a Review Voice review. - **example-reviewer**: Independent second look at a diff or a task's stated result, before it's marked done — the ROAST half of this project's CREATE → ROAST → IMPROVE discipline (see `Tasks/README.md`), run by someone other than whoever wrote the change. Use when a non-trivial change is ready to close out a `Tasks/&lt;slug&gt;/` folder, or when asked to "review this diff", "roast this PR", "get an independent verdict before merging", or "double-check this before I call it done". Not for writing or fixing the code itself, and not for deciding what to build next — see "What this is not" below. - **exarchos-fixer**: Use this agent when a task has failed and needs diagnosis and repair with adversarial verification. &lt;example&gt; Context: A delegated task failed its quality gates or tests user: "Task-005 failed its test-adequacy gate — fix it" assistant: "I'll dispatch the exarchos-fixer agent to diagnose and repair the failure." &lt;commentary&gt; Failed task requiring root cause analysis and targeted fix triggers the fixer agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([lvlup-sw/exarchos](https://github.com/lvlup-sw/exarchos/blob/HEAD/rendered/agents/fixer.md) · ★24)
- **exarchos-implementer**: Use this agent when dispatching implementation tasks to a subagent in an isolated worktree — verification scales with the task's risk tier (the verification ladder), not a universal test-first ceremony. &lt;example&gt; Context: Orchestrator is dispatching a task from an implementation plan user: "Implement the agent spec handler (task-003)" assistant: "I'll dispatch the exarchos-implementer agent to implement this task on the verification ladder in an isolated worktree." &lt;commentary&gt; An implementation task at any verification tier triggers the implementer agent. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([lvlup-sw/exarchos](https://github.com/lvlup-sw/exarchos/blob/HEAD/rendered/agents/implementer.md) · ★24)
- **exarchos-reviewer**: Use this agent when performing read-only code review for quality, design compliance, and test coverage. &lt;example&gt; Context: Feature implementation is complete and needs review user: "Review the agent spec handler for code quality" assistant: "I'll dispatch the exarchos-reviewer agent to analyze code quality and design compliance." &lt;commentary&gt; Code review request triggers the reviewer agent for read-only analysis. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([lvlup-sw/exarchos](https://github.com/lvlup-sw/exarchos/blob/HEAD/rendered/agents/reviewer.md) · ★24)
- **exarchos-scaffolder**: Use this agent for low-complexity scaffolding tasks — file creation, boilerplate generation, and structural setup. &lt;example&gt; Context: Orchestrator needs new files or boilerplate created user: "Create the directory structure and stub files for the new feature" assistant: "I'll dispatch the exarchos-scaffolder agent to generate the scaffolding in an isolated worktree." &lt;commentary&gt; Simple file creation and boilerplate generation triggers the scaffolder agent with concise output. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([lvlup-sw/exarchos](https://github.com/lvlup-sw/exarchos/blob/HEAD/rendered/agents/scaffolder.md) · ★24)
- **excali-reviewer**: Read-only visual reviewer that loads the licensed excali-graphic quality contract from the AIEB connector and judges a rendered image after its mechanical gate passes. - **Excel Accessibility**: Excel workbook accessibility specialist. Use when scanning, reviewing, or remediating .xlsx files for accessibility.
